\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {\text {o p t}} (x, a) \left\{\pi^ {\text {o p t}} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\} d x \mathbb {i} (\mathcal {a} _ {0}) \tag {e.104} \\ { = } { \mathbb { e } \int _ { x } \sum _ { a \in \mathcal { a } } q ^ { o p t } ( x , a ) \{ \pi ^ { o p t } ( a | x ) - \widehat { \pi } _ { \mathcal { i } } ( a | x ) \} \mathbb { i } ( x \in \mathbb { x } _ { * } ^ { c } ) d x \mathbb { i } ( \mathcal { a } _ { 0 } ) . } \\ \end{array}
$$

let  $\widehat{a}_{\mathcal{i}}(x) = \operatorname{arg}\max_{a} \widehat{q}_{\mathcal{i}}(a, x)$ . similarly, we can show the event  $\widehat{a}_{\mathcal{i}}(x) \notin \arg \max_{a \in \mathcal{a}} q^{opt}(x, a)$  occurs only when

$$
\max  _ {a} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) - q ^ {o p t} \left(x, \widehat {a} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x)\right) \leq 2 c | \mathcal {i} | ^ {- b _ {*}}. \tag {e.105}
$$

since  $\max_{a}q^{opt}(x,a) - q^{opt}(x,\widehat{a}_{\mathcal{i}}(x)) = \sum_{a}q^{opt}(x,a)\{\pi^{opt}(a|x) - \widehat{\pi}_{\mathcal{i}}(a|x)\}$ , we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \left\{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathbb {x} _ {*} ^ {c}) d x \mathbb {i} (\mathcal {a} _ {0}) \\ \leq 2 c | \mathcal {i} | ^ {- b _ {*}} \int_ {x} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathbb {x} _ {*} ^ {c}) d x = 2 c | \mathcal {i} | ^ {- b _ {*}} o (| \mathcal {i} | ^ {- b _ {*} \alpha}) = o (| \mathcal {i} | ^ {- b _ {*} (1 + \alpha)}), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first equality follows from a5. combining this together with (e.104) yields (e.102). the proof is hence completed.

# e.12 proof of theorem 4

for a given  $\varepsilon > 0$ , let  $\mathcal{a}_* = \{\max_a q^{opt}(x, a) - q^{opt}(x, \widehat{a}_{\mathcal{i}}(x)) \leq \varepsilon\}$ . notice that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {\text {o p t}} (x, a) \left\{\pi^ {\text {o p t}} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\} d x \tag {e.106} \\ = \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \left\{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*}) d x \\ + \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \left\{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*} ^ {c}) d x. \\ \end{array}
$$

using similar arguments in the proof of theorem 3, we can show

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*}) d x \leq \varepsilon \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*}) d x \\ \leq \varepsilon \lambda \left\{x: \max  _ {a} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) - \max  _ {a \in \mathcal {a} - \arg \max  _ {a ^ {\prime}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a ^ {\prime})} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \leq \varepsilon \right\} = o (\varepsilon^ {1 + \alpha}). \quad (e. 1 0 7) \\ \end{array}
$$

moreover, similar to (e.105), we can show the event  $\widehat{a}_{\mathcal{i}}(x) \notin \arg \max_{a \in \mathcal{a}} q^{opt}(x, a)$  occurs only when

$$
\max _ {a} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) - q ^ {o p t} (x, \widehat {a} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x)) \leq 2 \max _ {a \in \mathcal {a}} | \widehat {q} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x, a) - q ^ {o p t} (x, a) |.
$$

it follows that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*} ^ {c}) d x \\ \leq \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \frac {4 \max  _ {a \in \mathcal {a}} | \widehat {q} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x , a) - q ^ {o p t} (x , a) | ^ {2}}{\sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x , a) \left\{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \right\}} \mathbb {i} (x \in \mathcal {a} _ {*} ^ {c}) d x \\ \leq \frac {4}{\varepsilon} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \max _ {a \in \mathcal {a}} | \widehat {q} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x, a) - q ^ {o p t} (x, a) | ^ {2} d x \leq \frac {4}{\varepsilon} \mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} | \widehat {q} _ {\mathcal {i}} (x, a) - q ^ {o p t} (x, a) | ^ {2} d x \\ = o \left(\varepsilon^ {- 1} | \mathcal {i} | ^ {- 2 b _ {*}}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

combining this together with (e.106) and (e.107) yields that

$$
\mathbb {e} \int_ {x} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {o p t} (x, a) \{\pi^ {o p t} (a | x) - \widehat {\pi} _ {\mathcal {i}} (a | x) \} d x = o (\varepsilon^ {1 + \alpha}) + o (\varepsilon^ {- 1} | \mathcal {i} | ^ {- 2 b _ {*}}).
$$

the proof is hence completed by setting  $\varepsilon = |\mathcal{i}|^{-2b_{*} / (2 + \alpha)}$ .
